## Current authorization

**Approved scope: AUTH-04 — sign in with an email address or a username (21 October 2026).** Contributor: **Boey** (platform/auth owner), who approved the plan in chat and asked for a direct push to `main` after a further explicit approval (no PR). Branch `auth-04-username-login` from `origin/main` at `f5f8995`. Collision check: no username-login code, branch, commit or task record existed (`rg` over TASKS.md, `git log --all --grep=username`, local worktrees `admin-01-b-admin-controls` and `hub-01-3d-landing`); unpushed teammate work cannot be ruled out. Behaviour: `POST /api/auth/login` takes `{ identifier, password }` and still accepts the older `{ email, password }` (exactly one of the two keys). An identifier containing `@` follows the existing email rules and matches `emailNormalized`; otherwise it follows the existing username rule (`/^[a-z0-9_-]{3,32}$/i`, which cannot contain `@`) and matches the unique `usernameNormalized`, so both are case-insensitive single indexed lookups with no schema change. A malformed identifier is a 400 field error ("Enter your email address or username."). Unknown email, unknown username, wrong password and disabled accounts share one 401 ("Email, username or password is incorrect.") and the existing dummy Argon2id verification. The failed-login counter is now keyed on the matched account's email (or the typed identifier when none matches), so email and username attempts on one account share its five-failure budget; the per-IP login limit, origin/CSRF checks, Argon2id, session rotation and the AUTH-03 pending-verification branch are unchanged. Known tradeoff: after exhausting an account's budget from one IP, a 429 on the other identifier reveals that the two belong to the same account; the per-IP limit bounds such probing. Exact edits: `server/src/modules/auth/{policy,service,router}.js` (`parseLoginInput`, `findLoginAccount`, `login(input, account)`), the auth README, `client/src/features/auth/{components/LoginForm.vue,store.js,api.js}` (field `login-identifier`, label "Email or username", `type="text"`, `autocomplete="username"`, `autocapitalize="none"`, `spellcheck="false"`), the auth unit/integration tests and the login-form selectors in `tests/e2e/{auth,cohorts,recovery,admin-workspace}.spec.js`. Verification on the branch: server auth unit 53/53 passed; auth integration 21 passed, 2 skipped (new: login by email and username in mixed case, identical 401 for unknown email/unknown username/wrong password, shared failure budget reaching 429); client auth unit failures are only the known pre-existing continuation, pixel-wipe and sidebar-copy cases, with the new label/payload test passing; E2E `auth.spec.js` 4/4 including a new sign-in by upper-cased username, `admin-workspace.spec.js` 2/2; `cohorts.spec.js` desktop and `recovery.spec.js` "instructors issue links" (line 448) also fail on an untouched `f5f8995` checkout, so they are not caused by this change; ESLint clean on changed files; `git diff --check` clean. `tests/e2e/auth.spec.js`, `continuation.test.js` and the auth README already failed Prettier at `f5f8995`. Boey approved the push and it landed on `main` as `e1db5ca` on 21 October 2026 (fast-forward, no PR). Next action: formal Review; a dedicated security review of the auth surface is planned for later.

**Approved scope: AUTH-03 — sign-up email-confirmation step (19 October 2026).** Eric directly requested this in chat and chose, after the question checkpoint, to reuse the existing COH-01 class-code onboarding, to count existing accounts as verified, and to own the shared auth edits. Boey has not reviewed or approved it; formal Review is pending. Branch `auth-03-email-verification` from `main` at `525066d`, uncommitted. **Project demonstration only: no real email, provider, credential, dependency or configuration change.** A six-digit `crypto.randomInt` code is printed to the server terminal by a console mailer; only its HMAC is stored. Collision check: no email-verification, mailer or verification route existed in code, `TASKS.md` or the README; remote branch tips were not inspected for unpushed teammate work. Exact shared edits: `server/src/models/User.js` (`emailVerifiedAt`, hidden `emailVerification`), `server/src/modules/auth/{router,service,session,rate-limits}.js` (202 register/login, two verify routes, 30-minute pending session, verification IP limit, unconfirmed accounts refused a session), `server/src/app.js` (`mail.mailer` option), `server/src/server.js` and `playwright.config.js` (test-only file mailer via `TEST_MAIL_OUTBOX` when `NODE_ENV=test`), and for the 8-bit screen wipes into the main tabs after signing in or up, or clicking a tab (requested by Eric, 19 October 2026; per-tab variants requested the same day) `client/src/App.vue` (mounts `PixelWipe.vue` once) and `client/src/main.js` (installs `installPageWipes(router)`, a router hook, so arriving at a main tab from another page plays that tab's own wipe: town diagonal, profile shutters, progress rising bars, classes and leaderboard checkerboard; no cohort or page code is changed). New: `client/src/features/auth/{pixel-wipe.js,pixel-wipe.css}`, `components/PixelWipe.vue`, `components/PixelEmoji.vue` (each wipe holds the screen covered for 0.73 s so the card can be read (about 1.0 s of visible card; the first version held it for 1 s and ran about 2 s end to end), and a click or key press skips the hold; opening a game plays its own pixel-art double doors instead (they slide shut, hold with the card like a sign, then swing open; per-game door colour and emoji, labels from the game catalog, and a plain wooden door for any game with no entry; `pixel-wipe.js` now reads the game catalog, read-only), About Sharlock has its own ring wipe, every wipe now lasts about 1.6 s end to end (0.42 s cover, 0.73 s hold, 0.42 s clear, brought down from 1.7 s at Eric's request), and the panel shows its emoji, 🗺️ town, 🔍 profile, 📊 progress, 🎒 classes, 🏆 leaderboard, redrawn as a 32 × 32 hard-edged 8-bit sprite so it matches the pixel theme; checked by screenshot in the browser). The wipe was measured in a real browser: 160 blocks animating only `opacity` in one-millisecond steps from 0 to 528 ms, 588 ms to cover, navigation underneath, 588 ms to clear, no main-thread long tasks, and the overlay always returns to idle; a mid-reveal screenshot showed the stepped diagonal edge. With the hook installed, `auth.spec.js` passes on mobile and desktop, real clicks on Town map, Detective profile and Learning progress were observed in the browser playing their own wipe and label (and nothing when already in the tab), each pattern was frozen mid-animation and screenshotted to confirm it looks distinct, and thirteen router-level checks passed in plain Node (distinct patterns and durations, first load, arrival from another page, moves between tabs, in-tab moves, leaving to an ordinary page, sign-in redirect, reduced motion, a thrown guard). `portal.spec.js` has five failures (heading focus after choosing a building, and a stale `.auth-mascot` check on the sign-in pages) that occur identically with the hook disabled, so they are not caused by the wipe; their original cause was not investigated. **Frame rate could not be measured**: the automation browser tab was throttled to about 1 frame per second even when idle, so judge smoothness by eye on a normal window. New: `server/src/modules/auth/{mailer,verification,verification-service}.js`, `client/src/features/auth/components/{VerifyEmailPanel,AuthSteps}.vue`, `tests/e2e/fixtures/mail.js`. Also changed: `client/src/features/auth/{api,store,auth.css}`, both account pages, the server and client auth READMEs and the tests that register through the API or UI. Consumers: every test using `registerTestStudent` now confirms a code through a memory mailer; `authService.register` is unchanged for the e2e fixtures and admin tools. Verification on the branch: 17 new server unit tests plus the 28 existing auth unit tests passed; the auth integration files passed (18 passed, 2 skipped as before); with `--replica-set`, 89 of 90 cohort/auth/admin/recovery/audit tests passed together (the one failure, a cohort count in `workspace.test.js`, passed alone and reflects data shared across files in one combined run); on a standard run 90 passed and 7 failed in `lifecycle-races.test.js`, which is not gated on `--replica-set` and fails without it independent of this change; ESLint reported zero problems on every changed file; the client production build succeeded; formatting is clean on the files created or already formatted before this change. **Not verified:** the client Vitest files that mount Vue components could not run here because this checkout's root `node_modules` is damaged (a plain `vue` copy without `@vue/*`, `.ignored_*` leftovers) and needs `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`; a combined whole-suite `pnpm test:e2e` is not a valid check because the shared 60-per-15-minutes CSRF limit is exhausted partway through (429 on `/api/auth/csrf`, then every fixture login fails), which the earlier recorded runs avoided by running specs in small groups with `--workers=1`. **Browser verification:** `auth.spec.js` passed on mobile and desktop (`node scripts/run-with-test-db.mjs e2e --replica-set --workers=1 --project=mobile-chromium --project=desktop-chromium tests/e2e/auth.spec.js`, 2 passed), including a wrong code, the right code, onboarding, sign-out and sign-in; `recovery.spec.js` "admin recovery revokes sessions" passed on both. Eric's manual run found the step after the code laggy; the cause was a real bug, not only frame rate: the signed-in view sat inside an `out-in` `Transition`, so navigating to `/join-class` unmounted the page while Vue held a not-yet-mounted view and threw `Cannot read properties of null (reading 'type')`, leaving the card blank. The signed-in view is now outside the transition, sign-in state is derived from the store and step frames were raised. Two failures remain that this change does not cause: `recovery.spec.js` "instructors issue links" fails at "Close and clear link" in the admin screens (not touched here; its baseline result was not measured), and the original `auth.spec.js` mobile sign-out went through the Profile menu that commit `1f82a75` (17 October) stopped showing it in, so the spec now signs out from `/progress`. Next action: Eric repairs `node_modules` and reruns the client Vitest files, decides who owns the unrelated "instructors issue links" recovery failure, then requests Review. The optional pixel onboarding tour was proposed and declined; it is not part of this scope.

**Sidebar scrollbar offset on sign-in pages (19 October 2026, requested by Eric).** `client/src/router/index.js` only: `/login` and `/register` now set `hideAccountSummary: true`, the existing switch the Profile page uses (it also applies the compact `fit-sidebar` spacing). When the session check failed, the sidebar showed a duplicate "Your account / temporarily unavailable" box above the navigation; that pushed the sidebar past the window height, so it scrolled internally and the shared 18px pixel scrollbar took 18px of its 236px, cramping the text. The same message is already in the page's own card. The sidebar width itself (236px from 992px, 204px from 768px, `--sidebar-width` in the shared `main.css`) was not changed: it is a fixed shell token, not derived from the golden ratio (it is 15.4% of a 1536px window, near but not equal to 1/φ⁴ = 14.6%). Measured in the browser: in the normal state the sidebar content (659px) exactly fills its box with no scrollbar. The sign-in page's sidebar in its failed-session state was not reproduced here (the browser session was signed in), so that visual result is unverified, and a window shorter than the sidebar content would still scroll it.

**G-SOC page layout polish (19 October 2026, requested by Eric, owner of G-SOC).** In `client/src/games/social-engineering-simulator/sim.css` and one element moved in `SocialEngineeringSimulator.vue`: the game's container now spans the whole content area, "Back to town" is its direct child and sits at the top left of the pale-green area (its left edge is the area's left edge plus padding, measured), and everything else (title, intro, box, chat) sits in one centred column of the same width as before (860 px, 1100 px for the wide chat view) with the title on its own line 8 px above the box. Before, the game hugged the left edge with a large gap on the right, and in the laptop-height layout the title floated beside the link on one flex row. No component logic, engine or `llm/` change; the browser tests find the link by role, so its position does not matter to them. Checked in the browser; no automated test covers this layout.

**Approved continuation scope: LOG-01-B — administrator audit viewer (17 October 2026).** Boey approved this frontend-only continuation of the existing LOG-01-A audit API, sequentially before ADMIN-01-B and ADMIN-01-C, on branch `admin-01-b-admin-controls` based on `5234720a87f1748700ecbef13ab0d75c21555fbe`. Add a read-only `/admin` viewer over `GET /api/admin/audit-events`, with event-type and browser-local date filters bounded to the existing 90-day contract, 25 rows per page (maximum 50), readable event/outcome/time, expandable identifiers, loading/empty/error/retry/expired-access states, and stale-request cancellation. Unspecified date bounds are omitted so the server applies the current rolling 90-day window on every request. Do not add export, name lookup, or backend changes. Scope: `client/src/features/admin/components/AuditEventsPanel.vue`, `client/src/features/admin/api.js`, `client/src/features/admin/pages/AdminPage.vue`, `client/src/features/admin/admin.css` only if needed, and focused `client/src/features/admin/__tests__/admin.test.js` / `api.test.js` coverage. Reuse current admin workspace styling and the existing session/capability-protected no-store API. Implemented and verified on the branch: 15/15 focused client tests passed; ESLint, diff check and targeted Prettier passed. Boey authorized branch-only publication on 17 October 2026; this does not authorize a PR or main merge. Formal Review and Boey acceptance remain pending.

**Approved continuation scope: ADMIN-01-B — institution and class editing (17 October 2026).** Boey approved this scope to follow LOG-01-B after its focused checks pass, on `admin-01-b-admin-controls` based on `5234720a87f1748700ecbef13ab0d75c21555fbe`. Add allowlisted `PATCH /api/admin/institutions/:id` for institution name/status and `PATCH /api/admin/cohorts/:id` for class name/term/status, both with `expectedRevision`; institution slug/ID and cohort parent/ID remain fixed. Treat absent internal revision fields as zero for existing documents, reject stale edits with 409, and audit successful changes in the write transaction. Deactivating a class preserves memberships, assignments and history, revokes its invitation, and requires a fresh invitation after reactivation. An institution may be deactivated only after every child class is inactive; institution reactivation leaves child statuses unchanged. Preserve unrelated sessions. Serialize class creation/reactivation/deactivation against institution deactivation with a shared parent revision guard in `server/src/modules/cohorts/service.js`, and extend REC-01-B eligibility guards to institution/class status so reset eligibility cannot race those changes. Assignment choices require an active class and institution; previously assigned inactive classes remain visible as read-only entries and their IDs remain in replacement payloads, which the server accepts only for existing active assignments at the expected instructor revision. Scope: `server/src/modules/admin/policy.js`, `server/src/modules/admin/service.js`, `server/src/modules/admin/router.js`, `server/src/modules/admin/README.md`, `server/src/models/Institution.js`, `server/src/models/Cohort.js`, `server/src/modules/cohorts/service.js`, `server/src/modules/audit/policy.js`, `server/src/modules/recovery/guards.js`, `server/src/modules/recovery/README.md`, `server/tests/unit/admin/workspace.test.js`, `server/tests/integration/admin/workspace.test.js`, `server/tests/integration/cohorts/enrollment.test.js`, `server/tests/unit/recovery/password-reset.test.js`, `server/tests/integration/recovery/password-reset.test.js`, `client/src/features/admin/api.js`, `client/src/features/admin/components/InstitutionCohortForm.vue`, `client/src/features/admin/components/AssignmentEditor.vue`, `client/src/features/admin/components/AuditEventsPanel.vue`, `client/src/features/admin/pages/AdminPage.vue`, `client/src/features/admin/admin.css`, `client/src/features/admin/README.md`, `client/src/features/admin/__tests__/admin.test.js` and `client/src/features/admin/__tests__/api.test.js`. Phase 2 verification: 50 server unit tests and 21 focused client tests plus the stale-class regression passed; institution/class editing, lifecycle-race and cohort integration checks passed, including one nonzero-cohort regression. A recovery combined run had a transient fixture CSRF 404; the named recovery test passed on a fresh test database and will be rerun with phase 3. Boey authorized branch-only publication on 17 October 2026; this does not authorize a PR or main merge. Formal Review and Boey acceptance remain pending.

**Approved continuation scope: ADMIN-01-C — account status controls (17 October 2026).** Boey approved this scope to follow ADMIN-01-B after its focused checks pass, on `admin-01-b-admin-controls` based on `5234720a87f1748700ecbef13ab0d75c21555fbe`. Verification on the branch: 49 phase-three server unit tests; all 7 account integration cases passed across two focused runs; 16 existing workspace/recovery integration cases passed; 30 focused client tests and the client build passed; root lint reported zero errors and two existing warnings. The admin-workspace E2E passed in both mobile and desktop Chromium projects (desktop passed after correcting empty-table test readiness). **Approved continuation scope: AUTH-01-C — revised password and username policy.** On 15 October 2026 Boey explicitly instructed a minimum of 12 characters, one capital letter and one special character. Apply 12–128 well-formed Unicode code points, at least one Unicode uppercase letter and one punctuation/symbol character to new account passwords; whitespace is allowed but does not satisfy the special-character requirement. Login checks length/encoding only, retaining access for accounts created under the earlier composition-free rule. Boey also explicitly approved increasing the username maximum from 24 to 32 after the requested account exceeded the previous limit; apply the same 3–32 ASCII policy in validation, schema, terminal and form hints. ## Mandatory selection and overwrite rules

1. **Inspect before selecting.** Read Git status/diffs, relevant code/tests, task notes and available branch/PR history. Search IDs, names, concepts and aliases. A preview/prototype is partial implementation; an empty reserved directory alone is not.
2. **Stop on a collision.** Existing partial/complete code or an active claim disqualifies automatic takeover. Do not edit, refactor, replace, delete, continue or clone that feature. Report the exact evidence and ask for another unimplemented task or a new feature. General shared-ownership language from older records grants no exception.
3. **Offer two paths:** choose an unimplemented, unclaimed backlog feature that passes inspection, or propose a new custom feature and inspect it too. If all are done, ask for a new feature immediately. If remaining work is blocked/claimed/partial, say so; an independently available prerequisite can be considered.
4. **Clarify before code.** For each feature or separately approved feature-change plan, complete a focused question-and-answer checkpoint about the user journey, design, intended behavior and technical implementation. Agree learning/interaction rules, difficulty, state, components/props/emits, APIs, schemas, security, mobile accessibility, dependencies and acceptance checks. Build on settled answers, recommend defaults with tradeoffs, and record decisions and open questions in the plan/task record. During implementation, ask follow-ups only when new information could materially change experience, behavior, data/security, architecture, dependencies, acceptance or scope; pause affected work for required answers and continue independent approved work. Clarification does not replace scope approval or contributor agreement, and silence is not agreement. Present the plan and wait for explicit approval. Do not install, scaffold or claim while waiting.
5. **Recheck immediately before edits.** New overlap returns to step 2. Record the real contributor, branch, approved scope, paths, dependencies and collision evidence. Reuse shared contracts; only approved additive registration edits are included. Missing contracts require a separate prerequisite task.

The exact decision tree is in [agents.md](agents.md#2-mandatory-feature-selection-decision-tree). Current explicit user instructions take precedence for their stated scope. Boey explicitly replaced the DOC-05 handover-only instruction with direct G-NEWS implementation in this chat. The existing preview and declared integrations are authorized; new overlaps and other features remain subject to the guardrails.

## Board conventions

- **Backlog:** specified work, not approval or proof of availability.
- **In Progress:** approved implementation/documentation underway; contributor, scope and branch recorded.
- **Review:** stated scope ready with actual evidence; acceptance pending.
- **Blocked:** a concrete dependency or collision prevents the chosen scope.
- **Done:** accepted by the reviewer, with evidence retained.

Move the task's active record rather than duplicating it across board states. References and specification headings are not additional claims. P0/P1/P2 mean foundation/assessment, core value and optional work respectively; priority never overrides authorization.

**G-TAG — Botnet Tag (multiplayer infection tag)** · Status: **In Progress** · Contributor: **Boey** (implemented by Claude Code) · Branch: `g-tag-01-round-core` (cut from `main` = `origin/main` `cbefb51`, 31 October 2026) · Plan approved by Boey on 31 October 2026 with all recommended defaults (`.local-handover/BOTNET-TAG-PLAN-2026-10-31.md`); Boey states the team consents to widening the agents.md §4 `ws` line for this game.

- **Concept:** 3–8 classmates (server bots fill to 3) in a fenced dockyard arena; one Patient Zero (two at 7+) tags survivors into the botnet; survivors solve 10–20 s patch-station tasks (firewall, patch, process, hash, spoof, account) for 15 s immunity; 3-minute rounds. Saved per player in class context, no leaderboard in v1 (PvP ranking stays a team decision). Course links: CS440 `10-software-security.pptx` (worms, botnets), `1-basics.pptx` (botnets), `09a-network-security.pptx` (firewall, worm); no deck covers patching, so that part is a team design.
- **Collision check (31 Oct):** no code, branch, commit or claim (rg over client, server, tests, docs, TASKS, README; `git branch -a`; `git log --grep`); only the brainstorm lines in `docs/boey-todo.md`. Teammates' unpushed work not visible.

No networking or wiring yet. `pnpm exec vitest run server/tests/unit/botnet-tag` 61/61; eslint and prettier clean.
- **Docs impact:** module READMEs (client + server, presentation summary), README "Using the app" row, agents.md §4 `ws` line, DESIGN.md (arena HUD), CREDITS.md (music), attempts README (ruleset), this record.

**QA-04 — Lint and test-suite stability fixes** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Xin Lei** · Branch: `qa-04-test-and-lint-fixes` (cut from `origin/main` at `1fc72c7`, 30 October 2026).

- **Baseline (`main` `1fc72c7`):** `pnpm lint` reported 6 errors; `pnpm test:integration` failed 99 of 237 tests and skipped 68; `pnpm test:unit` failed 3 tests by timeout.
- **1 (`524fa10`) — lint:** `admin/service.js` `passwordMatches` no longer starts with a value every path overwrote before reading it; `tests/integration/admin/delete-account.test.js` loses three unused imports and an unused `tokenFrom` helper; `tests/unit/admin/workspace.test.js` drops the password field with `delete` instead of an unused rest variable. Behaviour unchanged; `eslint .` clean.
- **2 (`ee523f7`) — integration isolation:** files ran in parallel against one shared database, so one file deleted another's users and sessions (401s) and left rows others counted; the transactional admin files skipped and `lifecycle-races` failed without a replica set. New `server/tests/integration/setup-database.js` (the integration project's `setupFiles`) gives each file its own `_test` database on the shared test server; `test:integration` now passes `--replica-set` like `test:e2e`; integration `testTimeout` 10 → 20 s and `hookTimeout` 15 → 30 s (one class-results test takes 1.6 s alone but passed 10 s under full parallel load). README test notes updated.
- **3 (`7d4f3cf`) — unit timeouts:** `phishing-post-mortem/triage-generator.test.js` property checks walk 500 seeds × 60 rounds (4.4 s and 5.7 s alone), so the file allows 30 s with coverage unchanged; `features/auth/__tests__/auth.test.js` mocks `views/Dashboard.vue`, which the router imports statically and which loads the 3D hub engine, so the route-meta test takes 0.16 s instead of 4.6 s alone and over 30 s in the full suite.
- **Results (30 Oct 2026):** `eslint .` clean; `pnpm test:unit` 141 files, 1642/1642 in two consecutive runs; `pnpm test:integration` 27 files, 237/237 in three consecutive runs (about 1 minute).
- **Not changed:** `page-fit.spec.js` "fits the window: Detective profile" fails because SKIN-01's wardrobe makes `/profile` 1,696 px tall at 1280 × 720; whether Profile should fit one screen is left to the shared UI work. E2E suites were not rerun for these test-infrastructure and lint changes.
- **Docs impact:** README test notes (replica set and per-file databases), this record.
- **Merge / next action:** merged into `main` on 30 October 2026; the Profile page-fit question stays with the shared UI work.

**SKIN-01 — Achievements unlock Sharlock's wardrobe** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `skin-01-wardrobe` (cut from `npc-01-hub-npcs` at `26253d5`, 30 October 2026, upstream unset; edits `HubScene.vue` too) · Plan approved by Boey on 30 October 2026 after the agents.md §2D checkpoint (scarves, hats, monocle frames and a magnifying glass; wardrobe on `/profile`; per-game achievements). Extends LEARN-01; PRO-01 is unrelated.

- **Achievements:** LEARN-01's 5 stay; 6 added, derived on read: "first case closed" per scored game (Customs cleared, Tower cleared, Post sorted, Wire held, Talked down; practice counts) and "Against the clock" (a completed standard-mode attempt on a ruleset the server registry marks competitive; practice does not count; personal context is not excluded, Boey to confirm). `listCompletedAttempts` now also projects `mode` (one line, attempts service).
- **Catalog and API:** `server/src/modules/wardrobe/` allowlist, four slots with defaults (red scarf, deerstalker, gold monocle, no accessory) and 11 unlockables, one per achievement (table in the module README). `GET /api/me/wardrobe`; `PUT /api/me/wardrobe/:slot` with exactly `{ itemId }` (session, CSRF, origin, 60/min per user; 400 validation, 403 locked). Unlocks are re-derived from the actor's own completed attempts on every request; only equipped ids are stored (`User.wardrobe`, enum per slot, defaults for old accounts). `/api/auth/me` gains `wardrobe`. The profile PATCH still rejects `wardrobe`.
- **Client:** `WardrobePicker.vue` on `/profile` (fieldset + native radios per slot, locked text, polite live region, store updated only after a successful save); shared looks in `client/src/data/wardrobeLook.js`; `Dashboard.vue` passes `auth.user.wardrobe` to `HubScene` → `createHub` → `createSharlock`. New rig options `scarfColor`, `tweedColor`, `monocleColor`, `hat: 'detective-cap'`, `accessory: 'magnifier'` (merged into the torso mesh, no extra draw), all defaulting to today's look. Dev-only `?wardrobe=` override and `__hub.debug.sharlockLook()`.
- **Shared edits (consumers recorded):** `User` schema and `serializeUser` (`/me`, login, register), auth store DTO, `server/src/app.js` router mount, `Dashboard.vue` (one prop), `Profile.vue` (one component), attempts `listCompletedAttempts` projection, `/api/me/progress` (11 achievement keys).
- **Collision check (30 Oct 2026):** no skin, cosmetic or wardrobe code, route, test, branch or record; LEARN-01's achievement module was extended, not replaced. Boey owns progress, profile, auth and attempts.
- **Docs impact:** new `server/src/modules/wardrobe/README.md`, profile and hub READMEs, README "Using the app" (Wardrobe), DESIGN.md (Wardrobe), this record.
- **Verification (30 Oct 2026):** server unit 936/936 (wardrobe + progress 24); integration `wardrobe` 11, `progress` 5, `profile` 10, `auth` 26 + 2 existing skips, `attempts` 20 (each `--no-file-parallelism`); client vitest auth/profile/data 97/97 and hub 251/252 before a DTO test fix, then green; eslint clean; E2E `wardrobe.spec.js` 2/2 (personal SQL run unlocks the navy scarf, equip, reload, hub `sharlockLook()` reports it), `profile.spec.js` 4/4, `hub.spec.js` 33 + 1 skip. Not verified: the 3D look of the flat cap, magnifier and tweed tints (no close-up screenshot; check `/?wardrobe=scarf-navy,hat-flat-cap,monocle-silver,accessory-magnifier` in dev); the desktop profile screenshot.

**NPC-01 — Scripted townsfolk on the wharf** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `npc-01-hub-npcs` (cut from `hub-07-escape-clearance` at `c822408`, 30 October 2026, upstream unset; both edit `HubScene.vue`) · Plan approved by Boey on 30 October 2026 after the agents.md §2D checkpoint (optional talk at the door, full rigs with tier fallback, 6 game keepers + 3 wanderers). This approval lifts "NPC dialogue" from the HUB-01 unauthorized list for scripted hub NPCs only; the social-engineering conversational NPC and `llm/` stay separate.

- **NPCs:** customs officer (SQL), telegraph clerk (CLI), postmaster (Phishing), police constable (Clearance), publican (Social), news vendor (Times), plus fisherman, lamplighter and gull feeder. Data and lines in `client/src/features/hub/scenes/wharf/npcs.js`; spawn and animation in `scenes/wharf/townsfolk.js`. Each spot is walkable, more than 2.3 m from every door spot and more than 1 m off the auto-walk lanes (unit test); each has a 0.8 × 1.8 × 0.8 m collider post.
- **Look and performance:** desktop tier uses full `createCapybaraRig` rigs (size 1.2, role caps and scarves, per-NPC `idleSeed`; Sharlock unchanged); compact and low use `createCapybaraStatic` (`quality.js` `npcRigs`). Rigs beyond 25 m skip updates; they turn to face Sharlock within 4 m; only torso and head cast shadows. Dev draw calls: desktop 175 → 280, low 73 → 78 (software renderer; no real-GPU measurement).
- **Talk flow:** an NPC within 2.5 m (or a keeper while Sharlock is on its door ring) shows "Talk to <name>" beside Enter. The `NpcDialogue.vue` panel (non-modal `role="dialog"`) steps through 2-4 lines with Next, then Go in (keepers; enters the game the same way as Enter) or Bye/Close. Walking pauses while it is open, focus moves into the panel and back to Talk on close, "Talking to <name>" is announced, and Esc closes Keys legend → dialogue → selection in that order.
- **Lines:** drafted from each game's README concept line; teaching hooks only, no answers. Boey to edit; owners may want to see their keeper's lines.
- **Affected paths:** `client/src/features/hub/` (`HubScene.vue`, `engine.js`, `scenes/a-wharf.js`, `scenes/shared/{capybara,quality}.js`; new `scenes/wharf/{npcs,townsfolk}.js`, `NpcDialogue.vue`, `useNpcDialogue.js`, tests; README), `tests/e2e/hub-npcs.spec.js` (new).
- **Collision check (30 Oct 2026):** no NPC code, route, test, branch or record existed (`rg` for NPC/townsfolk across client, server, tests, docs; DESIGN.md and TASKS.md listed it only as unauthorized). Boey's own hub module.
- **Docs impact:** hub README ("Townsfolk (NPC-01)"), README "Using the app" (Talk control), DESIGN.md (3D hub landing: authorization and panel rules), this record and the HUB-01 follow-up lines.
- **Verification (30 Oct 2026):** vitest `client/src/features/hub` 17 files 155/155; eslint clean; E2E `hub-npcs.spec.js` 6/6 (talk → lines → Go in opens the game; Esc closes and returns focus; a wanderer's line), `hub.spec.js` 33/33. Two screenshots (1280×800, 375×812): no horizontal scroll at 375px, but Talk and Enter wrap to two rows and a long Enter label slightly overlaps the Keys button. Not verified: a real GPU, NPC placements other than the telegraph door by eye, whether the news vendor's collider post snags a player walking the quay (the E2E teleports to the fisherman because a straight-line debug walk caught on it).
- **Follow-up (29 Oct 2026, Boey's "Fix both first"):** browser QA found the news vendor's post (x -3.0 to -2.2) overhanging the pier rail at x -2.9, pinning a player who walked north along the rail. The vendor moved to x -1.6, leaving a 1 m gap to the rail; held-key walks north from x -2.6, -1.6 and -0.8 now all pass the vendor and stop only at the kiosk. Browser QA also passed Talk on all six keepers and the 375px Talk/Enter row (no overlap measured); the wanderers and the cosmetics close-up were not checked by eye. **CODE-27 — CLI readability pass** · Status: **Review** · Maintenance contributor and feature owner: **Xin Lei** · Branch: `code-27-cli-readability` (cut from `origin/main` at `740e58b`, 29 October 2026).

- **A (`886d240`):** `service.js` `submitCommand()` (about 90 lines, four branch levels, a nested ternary) now calls `applyCommand()`, which delegates to `runLookup()`, `confirmStep()` and `recordMistake()`; each returns `{ outcome, message, closed, penalty }`.
- **B (`adf6813`):** `completeRun()` and the saved-attempt ruleset share `outcomesByTopic()` in `ruleset.js` (topics read from each incident, so injected scenarios behave as before) instead of two copies; `snapshot()` reads the current incident once, uses one `isResolved()` filter and `runStatus()` instead of a nested ternary; `router.js` `route()` takes one `handler`.

- **Invariants:** identical API responses, messages, error codes, scoring and streaks, time penalties, idempotent action IDs, pause/resume, timers, request cancellation, saved-attempt state and `cli-3` rules; no UI text change.
- **Tests (29 Oct 2026, Xin Lei's Windows machine):** baseline before edits: CLI server unit 88/88, CLI client vitest 44/44. After: CLI + attempts server unit 128/128; integration `cli-cyber-defender` + `attempts` 34/34; CLI client vitest 44/44; eslint clean on both modules; prettier applied to edited files. E2E `cli-cyber-defender.spec.js` (after `pnpm exec playwright install chromium`): default workers gave 44 passed / 9 timed out / 1 skipped while `pnpm dev` was also running (every failure a 30 s timeout or context teardown timeout, none an assertion on output); rerun with `--workers=2`: **53 passed, 1 skipped**, matching CLI-03's recorded result.

**SQL-02 — SQL Injection Arcade as a run of generated cases (ruleset `sql-2`)** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** (module owner: Keane) · Branch: `sql-02-arcade-run` (cut from `origin/main` at `c070ac8`, 29 October 2026, upstream unset) · Plan approved by Boey on 29 October 2026 (overnight batch with HUB-05, CLI-03, HUB-06; Boey chose all five case types and the PHISH-01 mode pattern).

- **Owner agreement:** Boey reports Keane's redesign is covered by the team owner agreement (`docs/boey-todo.md` "Waiting on" and §D); the agreement text itself is not in the repo. Boey to notify Keane. `llm/` untouched.
- **Rules (`sql-2`):** seeded, deterministic Customs House cases (`generator/`, SHA-256 stream, `GENERATOR_VERSION` `sql-gen-1`; answers server-only): T1 login bypass (quoted `OR`, comment cut-off on a named account, unquoted numeric field), T2 UNION column matching on a fictional manifest search, T2 boolean-blind (true/false probe pair, then `SUBSTRING(col,i,1)` probes and a yes/no answer), T3 second-order (register a stored name, then an unsafe change-password update must hit exactly the target row), and a repair choice after every four cases (only parameterise is correct). Ramp: cases 1-3 T1, 4-7 T1/T2, 8+ T2/T3. Class runs: timed 180 s, 3 lives, competitive. Personal runs: untimed practice, 12 cases plus 3 repairs, server-recorded hints mark a case assisted. 3 tries per case (blind: 6 probes plus the answer); unsupported syntax counts as a try. Points 10/20/30 by tier, +10 first try when not assisted, 10 per correct repair; a wrong repair scores 0 and costs no life. `maxActions` 210, `maxScore` 1130. The second-order stored name (≤ 40 chars) sits in attempt state only until the update runs.
- **Parser:** `engine.js` tokenises (≤ 48 tokens, strings ≤ 80 chars) and evaluates an allowlist grammar over in-memory synthetic rows; anything else is UNSUPPORTED, SYNTAX_ERROR or COLUMN_MISMATCH with teaching feedback. No SQL is executed.
- **Shared edit:** `server/src/modules/attempts/production-registry.js` +2 lines registering `sqlLegacyRuleset` (`sql-1`, `current: false`, new `legacy.js`). `sql-2` class runs rank on the timed Best board ("Timed runs"), separate from `sql-1`.
- **Affected paths:** `server/src/modules/games/sql-injection-arcade/` (new `engine.js`, `generator/`, `policy.js`, `scoring.js`, `feedback.js`, `legacy.js`; `ruleset.js` and README rewritten; `parser.js` kept for `sql-1`); `client/src/games/sql-injection-arcade/` (view and composable rewritten; new `RunHud`, `CasePanel`, `AttemptForm`, `BlindAnswer`, `HintPanel`, `ResultPanel`, `RunReview`; `MockAdminLogin` and `QueryExplanation` removed; How to play, CSS, README); `tests/e2e/fixtures/sql.js` (new shared `playSqlRun`, reads `state.seed` from the test DB); `sql-injection-arcade`, `class-results`, `leaderboard` (caption now "Timed runs") and `progress` specs; README "Using the app" row and paragraph; README and TASKS G-SQL blueprint update notes; attempts README.
- **Collision check (29 Oct 2026):** only `origin/g-sql-saved-arcade` matches, an ancestor of `main`; SQL-01, HOW-02 and FONT-04 are merged; no open SQL branch or claim.
- **Docs impact:** server and client SQL READMEs (presentation summary), README "Using the app" row and G-SQL paragraph, README and TASKS G-SQL blueprint copies (identical SQL-02 update note), attempts README, this record.
- **Tests (29 Oct 2026):** server unit (engine, generator, ruleset, legacy, attempts) 101/101; integration `sql-injection-arcade/game.test.js` 6/6 and `attempts/` 20/20; client vitest 24/24; eslint clean; E2E `--workers=1`: `sql-injection-arcade.spec.js` 13 passed, 1 skipped (desktop-only scrollbar test), `class-results` 2/2, `leaderboard` 4/4, `progress` 4/4, `page-fit` 12 passed / 12 skipped, `portal` 20/20.
- **Not verified:** screenshots or visual review; a class run ending by the real 180 s deadline (the E2E ends it by lives); Keane's review.

**HUB-05 — 3D destination pick drops before Enter** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `hub-05-destination-drop` (cut from `origin/main` at `c070ac8`, 29 October 2026, upstream unset) · Plan approved by Boey on 29 October 2026 (overnight batch with CLI-03, SQL-02, HUB-06).

- **Bug:** a list or minimap pick flew to the building, then dropped back to free roam and the game could not be entered (Boey, 29 Oct, from the west promenade).
- **Cause (reproduced in Playwright):** `routeToDoor` started the auto-walk at the nearest lane node without a walkability check; the west lane end (-24, 36) sits inside the crate collider, so the walk stalled, the stall rule cleared route and camera hold, and the doors' steer-away check dropped the pick. A second bug: a pick during the enter swoop overwrote the enter flight and left the hub stuck in `entering`.
- **Fix:** routes start and end only at walkable lane nodes (`scenes/shared/player.js`); a stalled route keeps the hold and the pick (`wasStalled()`, `scenes/shared/doors.js`), so Enter stays usable; picks are ignored outside `explore` (`doors.js`, `engine.js`, `HubScene.vue`). Steering by hand still drops the pick.
- **Collision check (29 Oct 2026):** Boey's own hub module; no other branch or record touches the destination flow.
- **Docs impact:** hub README (new "Destination picks" section, two stale lines fixed), this record.
- **Tests:** `tests/e2e/hub.spec.js` +2 (pick from the west promenade then Enter; pick during the swoop is ignored); both failed against the old code on desktop and mobile. No unit test: the stall rule lives inside Babylon physics.
- **Verification (29 Oct 2026):** vitest `client/src/features/hub` 12 files 109/109; eslint on changed files clean; E2E `hub.spec.js` + `hub-auto.spec.js` 37 passed, 5 skipped (existing project skips), `--workers=1`. One earlier run had a flaky 'deselects a destination' failure (`__hub.debug` null), passed on rerun; not investigated. Only the CLI destination was driven from the promenade; others share the routing but were not each checked.

**LORE-01 — Lore intro for the 3D hub** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `lore-01-intro` (cut from `origin/main` at `114468b`, 28 October 2026, upstream unset) · Plan approved by Boey on 28 October 2026 after the agents.md §2D checkpoint (all recommended defaults accepted).

- **Behaviour:** on first entry to the 3D hub on `/`, white serif title cards fade in and out on black (five cards, Boey's text, 23 Oct spec), then the veil fades up on the existing spawn camera at the wharf. Hold per card = max(1.5 s, 0.32 s × words; raised from 0.25 s on 29 Oct 2026 at Boey's request), 0.4 s fades, about 27 s in all. Enter, Space or a click advances one card; Esc or the visible Skip ends it. Reduced motion: no fades, no auto-advance, plain cards with Continue and Skip. Real DOM text in an `aria-live` region; dialog focus trap with focus restored to the hub. If the scene is not ready when the cards end, the last card shows "Loading the wharf…" until it is. Hub input and `HubAmbience` stay paused while it plays. A "Replay intro" button sits beside Reset camera. 2D mode and the no-WebGL fallback never show it.
- **Seen state:** `localStorage` key `sharlock.loreIntroSeen` (non-sensitive, per browser), set on finish or skip, with an in-memory fallback when storage throws. A per-account flag was rejected as an unnecessary shared schema change.
- **Font:** `@fontsource/libre-baskerville` 5.3.0 (OFL-1.1), weight 400, pinned exact, imported only by the intro component.

**SAVE-01 — Saved attempts and class leaderboards for every game** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `save-01-game-adapters` (cut from `origin/main` at `36d8f77`, 27 October 2026, upstream unset). Plan: `.local-handover/SAVE-01-PLAN-2026-10-27.md` (local, not shared). Sub-stages: SAVE-00 shared server-recorded path (`e92a4bf`), SAVE-CLEAR (`b68ce5d` server, `9fed1a1` client), SAVE-PHISH (`a4ed7f3` server, `04926f1` client), SAVE-CLI (`c682fb7` server, `112d873` client), SAVE-SOC (`65c2517` server, `a970567` client); test fixes `57fa5ed`, `c922bdf`, `5b1ed30`.

- **Owner agreement (27 October 2026):** Boey reported that Russell (G-CLEAR), Xin Lei (G-CLI), Eric (G-SOC) and Athithya (G-PHISH) each gave explicit permission for Boey to edit their games to add saving. Athithya also consented to a fresh adapter replacing his unmerged `origin/g-phish-02-continuation` and `origin/g-phish-03-generated-cases` work and said he will delete those branches himself. The `llm/` engine is untouched.
- **Collision evidence:** those two phishing branches were the only unmerged game work; the `g-clear`, `g-cli` and `g-soc` remote branches had 0 commits ahead of `origin/main`.
- **Decisions:** (1) CLI and Social are server-recorded: their live engines keep timing and LLM state in server memory, so the server records the computed result once through `recordCompletedAttempt`, idempotent per run, and the public start and action routes return 409 `ATTEMPT_SERVER_RECORDED`. (2) Clearance: one attempt is one six-level climb, `submit-level` once per level, max 600, practice best. (3) Phishing: the server picks a random case, one `submit-verdict`, max 100, practice best; the old `/case` and `/verdict` routes are removed. (4) CLI: timed runs save as standard and competitive (Best tab "Timed runs"), practice saves as practice (all-time only), max 2100. (5) Social: practice, max 100, the chat stays in memory, sessions bound to the player. CLI runs and Social sessions now require sign-in and are owner-bound (previously anonymous). Also fixed a latent attempts model bug: ruleset identity validators were shared across discriminators, which broke every game once a second ruleset registered.
- **Verification so far:** SAVE-00 attempts, leaderboards, progress and class-results integration 51/51; Clearance unit 34/34, integration 5/5, client 23/23, E2E 24 passed 2 skipped; Phishing server unit 51/51, integration 13/13 (with Clearance and SQL), client 20/20, E2E 25 passed 1 skipped; CLI server unit 62/62, integration 43/43; Social server unit 202/202, integration 38/38.
- **Verification (27–28 October 2026):** CLI client 30/30 and E2E spec 42 passed (one flaky mobile pause test passed alone); Social client 92/92 and new E2E spec 4/4 (chat replies stubbed with `page.route`; `playwright.config.js` gives the e2e API placeholder LLM settings so `/session` starts, no upstream call is made). `pnpm test:unit` 1242/1242 (exit 0); full integration 195/195 after adding cleanup to the Clearance and Phishing suites; `pnpm build` passes; `pnpm lint` errors only under `.local-handover/`. Full `pnpm test:e2e`: 200 passed, 19 failed, 21 skipped; stale `progress.spec.js` and `auth.spec.js` expectations were fixed, and the rest passed when rerun alone except the session cascade below.
- **E2E session cascade (fixed 27 October 2026, test-only):** `signedInPage()` in `tests/e2e/fixtures/class.js` opened its context with `browser.newContext()`, which inherits the worker's `storageState` cookie, so the second user's login rotated and deleted the shared worker session and later tests got 401 (for example `news.spec.js:65`). The fixture now passes an empty `storageState`; no server bug. Rechecked with one desktop worker: CLI + news 30 passed; Clearance + Phishing + Social + news 36 passed.
- **Known issues:** the shared chooser's wording ("Save this case to", "Open the case") reads oddly in CLI and Social. CLI fetches a fresh CSRF token per command, adding a request inside timed windows. CLI CSRF fetch per command: addressed by SAVE-03. (Dev-browser and 375 px check: see Manual check below.) Chooser wording: addressed by SAVE-02.
- **Waiting on Eric (G-SOC):** live Social Engineering Simulator play needs Eric's LLM API key. Eric to share it privately with the team (never in chat logs, docs, commits or screenshots); each player sets `LLM_API_KEY` and `LLM_MODEL` in their own git-ignored root `.env` (see README §2). Until then the game shows its "not configured" message; the e2e placeholder in `playwright.config.js` only marks it configured for stubbed tests and makes no upstream call.
- **Merge (27 October 2026):** commit `3e80732` fast-forwarded into `main` with Boey's approval, together with this record update; no PR. The branch is pushed to `origin`. The dev-browser and 375 px checks were not done before the merge.
- **Full E2E on `main` (27 October 2026, `dd55bdd`, after the fixture fix):** `pnpm test:e2e` 212 passed, 7 failed, 21 skipped (11.1 min, exit 1). Every failure passed when rerun alone with `--workers=1`: mobile `activation.spec.js:83` and `cohorts.spec.js:20` 3/3 (both had failed in the shared admin sign-in, `tests/e2e/fixtures/admin.js:26`, "Could not authenticate the E2E session"); desktop `hub.spec.js` 11 passed 1 skipped (`:142`, `:198` had timed out); desktop `sql-injection-arcade.spec.js` 4/4 (`:10`, `:65`, `:121` had timed out). No SAVE-01 spec failed. The load-dependent admin sign-in failure is unexplained (a login rate limit or slowdown late in the run is suspected, not confirmed), so the full run does not yet exit 0.
- **Manual check (27 October 2026):** Boey ran the dev-browser pass against the team Atlas `sharlock_dev` (admin, instructor, class with join code, students; each game personal and class; `/leaderboard`; CLI timed vs practice; Phishing completed screen; 375 px) and reported "all looks good". UX follow-ups found during the pass are tracked in Boey's local todo, not here. The four owners have been told their games now save.
- **Review decisions (27 October 2026):** Boey accepted, as-is, the agents.md G-SQL wording ("saved practice") and the Clearance Tower topic threshold (both levels of a model at 75 or more).
- **Next action:** acceptance by Boey.

**UI-09 — Sign-out lands on /login** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `ui-09-signout-redirect`, approved by Boey on 27 October 2026 (lunch batch).

- **Scope:** `auth.logout()` in `client/src/features/auth/store.js` only, plus tests and the auth README; server CSRF on logout is unchanged.
- **Root cause:** a lapsed `sharlock.sid` cookie (or a sign-out in another tab) leaves a stale in-memory CSRF token; `POST /api/auth/logout` returned 403 `CSRF_INVALID`, so the sign-out stayed put with "Your security check expired".
- **Fix:** on `CSRF_INVALID` the store refetches the token and retries logout once; success or 401 clears identity and the layout navigates to `/login`; a second failure keeps the identity and the error (a live session never looks signed out).
- **Collision check:** auth module is Boey's; no other branch touches `store.js` logout.
- **Checks:** client auth unit 68/68 pass; server auth integration 17 passed, 2 skipped (new case included); `tests/e2e/auth.spec.js` 6/6 pass (desktop and mobile, new lapsed-cookie sign-out test included); eslint and prettier clean on changed files. Not checked in a real dev browser.

**SAVE-03 — Cached CSRF token for attempt writes** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `save-03-attempt-csrf-cache` · Approved by Boey on 27 October 2026 (Round 1). Owner to notify: Xin Lei (CLI).

- **Scope:** `client/src/features/attempts/api.js` (`writeAttempt` caches the CSRF token in module memory, shares one in-flight fetch, and on a 403 `CSRF_INVALID` clears the cache, refetches and retries the same write once; a 401 clears the cache; every other error is rethrown unchanged); new `client/src/features/attempts/__tests__/api.test.js`; `client/src/features/attempts/README.md`. No server, store or CLI module edits; the auth store was not coupled (the retry covers session rotation on login/logout).
- **Safety:** `csrf.protect` runs inside `requireMutation` (`[requireOrigin, csrf.protect]`, `server/src/modules/auth/router.js:457`) before every attempt and CLI mutation handler (`server/src/modules/attempts/router.js` start/action/complete routes, `server/src/modules/games/cli-cyber-defender/router.js` `route()` guards), so a CSRF-rejected write never ran and the single retry cannot double-apply an action. Origin failures (`ORIGIN_FORBIDDEN`) are not retried.
- **Checks:** `pnpm exec vitest run --project client` over attempts plus the five game folders 173/173 passed (16 files, 6 new in `api.test.js`); eslint and prettier --check on `client/src/features/attempts` and TASKS.md clean; `tests/e2e/cli-cyber-defender.spec.js` 43 passed, 1 skipped.
- **CLI acceptance (30 October 2026):** the CLI Cyber Defender part is accepted by its module owner, Xin Lei. The other games' and shared parts stay in Review until their owners accept them.

**E2E-01 — Diagnosable, busy-tolerant E2E fixture sign-in** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `e2e-01-fixture-signin` · Approved by Boey on 27 October 2026 (lunch batch 3).

- **Scope (test-only):** new `tests/e2e/fixtures/sign-in.js` exports `postFixtureLogin`, which posts `/api/auth/login` with the existing Origin and CSRF headers. It retries only HTTP 503 with error code `PASSWORD_SERVICE_BUSY` (up to 3 retries, 200 ms × attempt plus 0–99 ms jitter). Any other failure throws immediately. The reused CSRF token stays valid because the gate runs after `csrf.protect` and before session rotation. The thrown message keeps the existing prefix and appends the status and code, e.g. "Could not authenticate the E2E session (503 PASSWORD_SERVICE_BUSY)." Only a code matching `^[A-Z_]{1,64}$` is shown, so no body text, passwords or cookies reach the error. `admin.js`, `auth.js` and `class.js` call the helper. No server, client or production limit changed.
- **Diagnosis:** the mobile admin fixture sign-in fails only under full-run load. The prime suspect is the Argon2 gate (`server/src/modules/auth/password.js`, max 2 concurrent operations, no queue), which answers an immediate 503 at the third concurrent hash. A 503 from the gate also counts toward the per-IP login failure budget (`loginIp`, 30 per 15 min in `server/src/modules/auth/rate-limits.js`), because `skipSuccessfulRequests` skips only successful responses. The per-account reservation is released on busy, not charged. Second suspect: the per-IP budget itself returning 429. Retries are not applied to 429 and are not used to bypass it. Before this change the fixtures discarded the status, so the two causes could not be told apart.
- **Not changed:** the production gate, its concurrency limit and the rate limits. Possible follow-up, needing Boey's approval: a short wait-queue in the gate, because about 30 simultaneous classroom logins would see 503s.
- **Docs impact:** none. `tests/e2e/README.md` does not exist, and README §2 does not document the fixtures.
- **Checks (27 October 2026):** `pnpm exec eslint tests/e2e/fixtures` exit 0; `pnpm exec prettier --check` on the four changed fixture files exit 0; `node scripts/run-with-test-db.mjs e2e --replica-set tests/e2e/admin-workspace.spec.js tests/e2e/cohorts.spec.js --workers=2` exit 0, 4 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped. Ports 3001 and 5174 were free before the run. No Playwright browser install was needed. The run did not prove that the retry path fired, because the load may not have produced a busy response.

**SQL-01 — SQL arcade desktop right-edge overflow** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `sql-01-desktop-overflow` · Approved by Boey on 27 October 2026 (lunch batch 3) under the recorded owner agreement. Owner to notify: **Keane**.

- **Scope:** `client/src/games/sql-injection-arcade/sql.css` only: the desktop override (`min-width: 768px` and `min-height: 600px`) drops its `100vw`-based `width` and `margin-inline`, so the base rule (`100%` plus the shell gutters, negative margins) sizes the frame from its container. Regression test "with a classic vertical scrollbar" added to `tests/e2e/sql-injection-arcade.spec.js` (desktop only; the file now launches without `--hide-scrollbars`); module README layout sentence updated. No shared CSS, layout, other game or server file touched. Docs impact: module README and this record.
- **Cause (measured, headless Chromium with `--hide-scrollbars` disabled and `html{overflow-y:scroll}`, 18px scrollbar):** `100vw` includes the scrollbar, so `.sql-game` right edge was 1431 vs `clientWidth` 1422 at 1440, 1191 vs 1182 at 1200 and 983 vs 974 at 992, giving page-wide horizontal scroll (`scrollWidth` 1431) and a clipped back link, indicator and score. After: 1422, 1182 and 974 (equal to `clientWidth`), `scrollWidth` equal to `clientWidth`, and 375px unchanged. Without a scrollbar nothing changes up to a ~1556px viewport; at 1556px and wider the frame is now capped at the shared 1320px content width and centred (was full-bleed), for Keane to judge.
- **Checks:** new scrollbar test (the spec now runs with `--hide-scrollbars` disabled and asserts `innerWidth - clientWidth > 0` first): with the two `100vw` lines temporarily restored, `node scripts/run-with-test-db.mjs e2e --replica-set tests/e2e/sql-injection-arcade.spec.js --workers=1 --project=desktop-chromium -g scrollbar` failed (exit 1, game right edge 983 vs 974 allowed); with them removed it passed (exit 0, 1 passed). Full run `node scripts/run-with-test-db.mjs e2e --replica-set tests/e2e/sql-injection-arcade.spec.js tests/e2e/page-fit.spec.js --workers=1`: exit 0, 21 passed, 0 failed, 13 skipped. `pnpm exec eslint tests/e2e/sql-injection-arcade.spec.js` and `prettier --check` on the spec, `sql.css`, module README and `TASKS.md`: exit 0.

**AUTH-07 — Password gate wait-queue** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `auth-07-password-wait-queue` · Approved by Boey on 27 October 2026 (lunch batch 3 follow-up; queue limits are the recommended defaults).

- **Scope:** `server/src/modules/auth/password.js` `createPasswordGate` keeps at most two concurrent Argon2id operations (memory bound unchanged) but now queues further callers in arrival order, up to 32 waiting, each for at most 5 seconds; only a full queue or an expired wait throws `PasswordBusyError` (`503 PASSWORD_SERVICE_BUSY`, unchanged shape and client message). A freed slot passes straight to the next waiter; wait timers are cleared and unref'd. Before, a third simultaneous hash failed at once, so about 30 students signing in together would have seen busy errors. Tests: `server/tests/unit/auth/password.test.js` replaces the no-queue case with queue, order, full-queue and timeout cases. Docs impact: auth server README (password operations paragraph) and this record.
- **Consumers:** `hashPassword`, `verifyPassword` and `verifyDummyPassword` (login, registration, activation, recovery, fixtures that hash directly); the client message in `client/src/features/auth/api.js` is unchanged. Rate limits are unchanged.
- **Checks (27 October 2026):** `pnpm exec vitest run --project server server/tests/unit` exit 0 (password file 5/5); `node scripts/run-with-test-db.mjs integration server/tests/integration/auth --no-file-parallelism` 26 passed, 2 skipped, exit 0; eslint clean on changed files; `prettier --check` clean on the code and test (the auth README already failed prettier on `origin/main` in an existing table, left unchanged).

**E2E-02 — Full E2E run under load** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `e2e-02-load-scheduling` (cut from `origin/main` at `12a6a90`, 27 October 2026). Approved by Boey on 27 October 2026.

The 7 failures pass alone with one worker (70 passed / 2 skipped), so the cause is CPU contention at five workers. Hub specs (240 s budgets, software WebGL and animation-frame waits) timed out; `admin-workspace.spec.js` (mobile journey) and `cohorts.spec.js` (desktop journey) exceeded the 30 s default by a few seconds. The CLI pause-on-leave failure was a real race, fixed separately in CLI-02, not load.
- **Scope:** `playwright.config.js` moves the hub specs (`hub.spec.js`, `hub-auto.spec.js`) into projects `hub-mobile-chromium` and `hub-desktop-chromium`, each with `workers: 1` (Playwright 1.63 supports `TestProject.workers`); `mobile-chromium` and `desktop-chromium` add `testIgnore` for them and keep their `use` objects. The global `workers` setting is unset. `tests/e2e/hub.spec.js` project-name checks now use `endsWith('mobile-chromium')`. `tests/e2e/admin-workspace.spec.js` and `tests/e2e/cohorts.spec.js` call `test.setTimeout(90_000)` in the long journeys, as `recovery.spec.js` does. Root tooling change: the team should be told that `--project=mobile-chromium` no longer includes hub specs; use `--project=hub-mobile-chromium` or `hub-desktop-chromium`.

**SAVE-04 — Attempt-start limits for shared addresses and browser tests** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `save-04-attempt-start-limits` · Approved by Boey on 27 October 2026 (recommended option: both changes).

- **Cause:** full E2E traces showed `POST /api/games/sql-injection-arcade/attempts` answering 429 with `ratelimit-policy: 60;w=900`, the per-IP start limiter. Every Playwright request arrives from one loopback address, and a real class behind one school address would share the same 60 starts per 15 minutes.
- **Scope:** `server/src/modules/attempts/router.js` exports `DEFAULT_ATTEMPT_LIMITS` and accepts a `limits` override; the production per-IP start limit rises from 60 to 300 per 15 minutes, every other limit is unchanged (30/user starts still bound abuse). `server/src/server.js` passes higher limits only when `NODE_ENV` is `test` (the Playwright API server); integration tests build apps directly and keep the defaults. Integration test: an injected `startIp: 1` answers 429 on the second start. Docs impact: attempts README limits paragraph and this record.
- **Checks (27 October 2026):** `node scripts/run-with-test-db.mjs integration server/tests/integration/attempts --no-file-parallelism` 20/20, exit 0; `pnpm exec vitest run --project server server/tests/unit` exit 0; eslint and `prettier --check` clean on changed files. Full E2E: pending the merged run.

- **Diagnosis:** the full run (218 passed, 6 failed) failed six tests that all pass alone. `portal`, `clearance-tower` and `cli-cyber-defender` long journeys hit the default timeout under load; `auth` mobile sign-out clicked before the offcanvas opened; `cohorts` clicked Generate while the class's enrollment-code GET was still loading, so the result was dropped as superseded; `news` called `update()` twice in one tick, and the second built from a stale `route.query`, resurrecting `q=passkey` (reproduced by the new unit test, which fails without the fix).
- **Scope:** test-only: `test.setTimeout(90_000)` in `tests/e2e/portal.spec.js`, `clearance-tower.spec.js`, `cli-cyber-defender.spec.js`; dialog-visible wait in `tests/e2e/auth.spec.js`; response waits in `tests/e2e/cohorts.spec.js` and `tests/e2e/news.spec.js`. App fixes (Boey-owned): `client/src/features/cohorts/components/EnrollmentCodePanel.vue` new `loading` prop disables revoke/replace/generate, wired in `pages/ClassManagementPage.vue`; `client/src/games/threat-briefing/composables/useNewsLocation.js` `update()` merges into a pending location. Tests: `cohorts/__tests__/components.test.js`, `threat-briefing/__tests__/newsLocation.test.js`. Docs: both module READMEs.
- **CLI leave/stay race (found during this task; Xin Lei's module under the owner agreement):** `askToLeave()` set `pausedForLeave` only after the pause request settled, so a quick "Keep playing" never resumed. `pause()` in `client/src/games/cli-cyber-defender/composables/useCliDefender.js` now returns `true` as soon as the local pause is set; `resume()` still waits for the in-flight request (CLI-02). New unit test: a timed pause reports `true` before its request settles. Owner to notify: **Xin Lei**.

**ADMIN-03 — Admins provision administrators** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `admin-03-provision-admins` · Approved by Boey on 27 October 2026.

- **Scope:** new `POST /api/admin/administrators` (`admin/router.js`, `admin/service.js` `provisionAdministrator`) reusing `parseInstructorInput`; recovery `issueActivation` and `activateAccount` now accept a disabled instructor or admin and record `activationRole` on the token (`recovery/model.js`); two new audit labels in `AuditEventsPanel.vue`; client `createAdministrator` (`admin/api.js`), `InstructorProvisionForm` gains a `role` prop, "Provision an administrator" section in the Instructors tab of `AdminPage.vue`; activation page and route title use neutral "STAFF ACCOUNT" copy. Reissue stays instructor-only; the status panel still never lists admins.

- **Security notes:** the role is fixed by the route (body is exact-object, extra fields 400); redemption requires the account to still hold exactly the role stored on the token, so a role change after issue fails closed (legacy tokens default to instructor); same session, CSRF/origin, transaction, admin re-check and `activationWrites` limiter (20/h/IP) as instructors; existing email or username gives the same safe 409; new audit events `admin.administrator.provision`, `admin.administrator.activation.issue`, `account.administrator.activate`; no role is revealed before token redemption.

**E2E-04 — Mobile sign-out settle wait** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `e2e-04-auth-signout-flake` (cut from `origin/main` at `f0e6a20`, 28 October 2026, upstream unset) · Approved by Boey on 28 October 2026.

- **Diagnosis:** the 28 October full run's only failure was `tests/e2e/auth.spec.js` mobile, clicking Sign out in the detective desk while the offcanvas was still sliding in (not stable, then not visible). Bootstrap sets `role="dialog"` at the start of `show()` and adds `show` (removing `showing`) only when the transition ends, so E2E-03's `toBeVisible()` wait could pass mid-animation.
- **Scope:** test-only: one local helper `signOutFromDetectiveDesk` in `tests/e2e/auth.spec.js` waits for the dialog to be visible with class `show` and without `showing`, used by both mobile sign-out paths. No app change.
- **Checks (28 October 2026):** `node scripts/run-with-test-db.mjs e2e --replica-set tests/e2e/auth.spec.js --project=mobile-chromium --project=desktop-chromium --workers=1` exit 0 four times (6 passed each; one alone with the list reporter, all three tests per project). `prettier --check` clean.
- **Docs:** no docs impact beyond this record: test-only change, no behaviour, setup or UI change.

**ADMIN-04 — Admin deletes a student account** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Spec branch: `admin-04-delete-account` (merged); build branch: `admin-04-delete-account-build` (cut from `origin/main` at `8ea6f0b`, 28 October 2026, upstream unset) · Spec approved by Boey on 28 October 2026; build approved by Boey on 28 October 2026 (overnight batch 7).

- **Why:** a student registered with the username `instructor`; admins can only Disable (reversible). Boey wants an irreversible delete.
- **Decisions (Boey, 27-28 October 2026):** admin only. v1 targets pure students only; instructors keep Disable (deleting them would orphan `Membership.approvedBy/removedBy` and `enrollment_codes.createdBy`); admins, mixed-role accounts and self are refused. Confirm by typing the username **and** re-entering the admin's password. Reason = required category (`wrong-name`, `duplicate`, `student-request`, `left-school`, `other`) plus a 10-500 character note required only for `other`; the dialog warns not to put names or emails in it. No email HMAC: a deleted student may re-register with the same email. The log is shown through the existing Audit tab filter, not a new tab.
- **Outcome rule:** no rows in `game_attempts` for the user → **hard delete** of `users`, `memberships` (`userId`), `auth_sessions` (`session.userId`) and `password_resets` (`userId`). Any attempt → **anonymise**: `username` = `deleted-<8 random hex>`, `emailNormalized` = `deleted-<userId>@invalid` (keeps the unique indexes), unusable `passwordHash`, default `avatarId`, cleared `howToPlayDismissed` and `emailVerification`, `accountStatus: 'deleted'`, `$inc` `authVersion`/`recoveryRevision`, memberships set `removed`, sessions deleted, reset links revoked. Attempts keep `playerId`.
- **API:** `POST /api/admin/users/:userId/delete` (state-changing, existing CSRF/origin mutation middleware, activation-writes limiter 20/hour). Exact-object body `{ confirmUsername, password, reasonCategory, reasonNote?, expectedRevision }`; 400 on extra fields. One transaction with the existing admin `withTransaction`; rechecks role, status and revision inside it; password checked through the Argon2 wait-queue before the transaction. Generic 403 on a wrong password, rate-limited. Response `{ outcome: 'deleted' | 'anonymised' }` only.
- **Audit:** new `admin.account.delete` in `auditEventTypes`; fields actor, target ID, target role, outcome, `reasonCategory`, `reasonNote`, timestamp; never email or username. Per-event retention: 180 days for this event, 90 for the rest (`audit/policy.js` retention map, `expiresAt` set in `audit/service.js`); the audit query window allows 180 days when filtering this event. Audit write failure rolls back the delete.
- **Shared schema change (integration-sensitive):** `User.accountStatus` enum gains `deleted`. Consumers checked 28 October 2026: leaderboards (`leaderboards/service.js:31-52`, all-time included), class results (`class-results/service.js:120-141`), roster (`cohorts/service.js:439-470`), recovery lookup, `findActiveSessionUser` and `resolveActor` already require `active`, so a deleted student never appears in rankings. Must change: admin `listUsers` (`admin/service.js:224-244`) hides `deleted`; `updateUserStatus` refuses it (no re-enable); `parseAccountStatusInput` never accepts it.
- **Client:** Accounts tab row action "Delete account" opening a dialog with the irreversibility warning, kept-results explanation, typed username, reason select + note, password; `AuditEventsPanel.vue` label for the new event.
- **Known limits:** Clearance Tower role names are player-typed (≤24 chars, `games/clearance-tower/policy.js:118-132`) and stay inside kept attempts; they are gameplay content, not identity, so they are left as-is. The default console mailer prints sign-up emails to server stdout (dev only). Audit rows keep the target ID until expiry.
- **Tests at build:** unit (body/reason parsing); integration with `--replica-set` (hard-delete path removes every listed row; anonymise path; refusals for self, admin, instructor, mixed-role, wrong username, wrong password, short/long note, stale revision, non-admin caller; deleted student absent from best and all-time leaderboards, class results and roster; same email re-registers; sessions revoked; audit row with 180-day `expiresAt`; audit failure rolls back); E2E admin deletes a student from the Accounts tab.
- **Docs impact at build:** `server/src/modules/admin/README.md`, `server/src/modules/audit/README.md`, `client/src/features/admin/` README if present, README "Using the app" admin row, this record.
- **Size estimate:** server stage (senior), client stage (senior), tests (worker), checks (worker): about 5-6 subagent runs.
- **Build decisions (Boey, 28 October 2026):** disabled students can also be deleted; an already-deleted target returns 404 and cannot be re-enabled through PATCH; `reasonNote` is stored as the audit module's one bounded free-text exception (dialog warns against names/emails); audit query window cap raised to 180 days for all queries (default stays 90; the delete filter requests 180). Audit outcome field is `accountOutcome` because `outcome` already means success/refused/failure. Anonymised accounts get a real Argon2 hash of a random secret (computed before the transaction) so login returns the generic failure, not a 500.
- **Built:** server `admin/{policy,router,service}.js`, `audit/{policy,model,service}.js`, `User.accountStatus` gains `deleted`, `listUsers` hides deleted rows; client `DeleteAccountDialog.vue`, Accounts row action (pure students only), Audit tab "Account deleted" label and note line, `AdminApiError.fieldErrors`; E2E fixture exposes `administratorIdentity`.
- **Verified (28 October 2026):** `delete-account.test.js` 11/11 and admin + audit integration 49/49 (`--replica-set`); `pnpm test:unit` exit 0, 121 files, 1361/1361; `vitest run client/src/features/admin` 50/50; `tests/e2e/admin-delete-account.spec.js` 2/2 (desktop + mobile); eslint and prettier on changed files clean.
- **Known gaps:** the leaderboard-absence test uses a raw attempt row (roster, class results and game list checked; best/all-time rankings rely on the existing `active`-only student filter in `leaderboards/service.js`); the E2E pages through Accounts to find its student (no username filter).
- **Next action:** Boey reviews; merge only with his OK.

**FONT-01 — Readable body font** · Status: **Review** · Contributor: **Boey** · Branch: `font-01-readable-body` (cut from `origin/main` at `f0e6a20`, 28 October 2026, upstream unset) · Approved by Boey on 28 October 2026 (shared design change).

- **Why:** Pixelify Sans was the body font site-wide and is hard to read at the town's 12-14px sizes. Boey compared four sets in a local mockup (`.local-handover/font-mockup-2026-10-28/`) and chose to keep Pixelify Sans for headings and the brand and use **DotGothic16** (dot-matrix, OFL-1.1) for body and UI text.
- **Scope:** `client/package.json` + `pnpm-lock.yaml` add `@fontsource/dotgothic16` 5.3.1 and `@fontsource/pixelify-sans` 5.3.0 (exact pins; both fonts now self-hosted, Google Fonts import removed from `main.css`; latin and latin-ext subsets only). `client/src/assets/styles/main.css`: `--font-body`, `--bs-body-font-family` points at it, pixel headings scoped to shared pages (skips `.is-utility` and the five game roots so game headings keep inheriting their own font). `town.css` `.is-town`, `HowToPlayDialog.vue` fallback and the Times dialog (`threat-briefing/news.css`, Boey's module) use `--font-body`. No game module of another owner changed.
- **Not changed (follow-ups):** Clearance Tower and Social Engineering still use `--font-pixel` as their game text font (owner choice); CLI and SQL still load Silkscreen from Google Fonts at runtime (game-owned). DotGothic16 has one weight, so bold body text is browser-synthesised.
- **Checks (28 October 2026):** `pnpm --filter @sharlock/client build` exit 0 (font assets 5-16 kB each); eslint on `HowToPlayDialog.vue` exit 0; vitest `game-palette.test.js` + `client/src/components` exit 0 (25/25); prettier check on changed files clean; browser at 375px and 1024px on `/login` and `/register`: headings Pixelify Sans 700, labels/paragraphs/buttons DotGothic16, both fonts loaded locally, no Google Fonts requests, no horizontal scroll, synthesised bold readable. Not checked in a browser: signed-in pages, game headings, hub HUD, 1440px.
- **Docs:** DESIGN.md typography, type scale and credits; CREDITS.md fonts; README "When adding UI"; `client/src/features/auth/README.md` and `client/src/features/cohorts/README.md` font mentions.
